Direct unimodular vertex charts `(u,t)->(u,ut)`, `(s,v)->(sv,v)` identify the exceptional `P¹` with normal `O(-1)`. At `ell=d` the polytope is one-dimensional and the character variety is the rational normal curve: no false extension of the full-dimensional smooth theorem. > **2026-11-06 — matrix increment: bounded-factor/non-pluripolar divisor-base distinction (§13.4).** **POME-NPP-DIVISOR-BASE-LOSS-001** (low / **verified conditional deduction**, original Lemma 13.4.2 and Proposition 13.4.3 both correct). On the bidisc \(u=\log|z_1|^2\), \(v=|z_2|^2\), the mixed non-pluripolar product vanishes **at each truncation level**, while the ordinary mixed current is \(\pi^{-1}\beta_2\wedge[\{z_1=0\}]\), of exact mass \(r^2\) on a divisor disk of radius \(r\). A local pure logarithmic divisor factor always kills the non-pluripolar product, even with bounded/smooth other factors; substituting the **ordinary** current for the printed non-pluripolar base \(S\) is false. The same example shows why §13.4.2 requires a base without polar mass. # Coverage increment — 2026-11-04 — exact unbounded complex Fourier pi/epsilon tensor-norm gap

**Reviewed production main:** `b6933b772f41b45918f7e25ed1afbbb1fd892272`. **POME-TENSOR-FOURIER-PI-EPS-GAP-001** (**low / verified conditional deduction; original propositions correct**). For each odd `m>=3`, an explicit complex Fourier matrix tensor in `ell^1_m tensor ell^1_m` has exact projective norm `m^2` and exact injective norm `m^(3/2)`, so the same-space norm ratio is `sqrt(m)` and unbounded. The proof uses coordinate cost, Fourier orthogonality and a quadratic-chirp Gauss-sum calculation valid for every odd composite `m`; no external Gauss-sum theorem or analytic limit. This rules out a false **unprinted** comparison, not the two different correct isomorphisms in the current reading edition.

**Reviewed production main:** `b6933b772f41b45918f7e25ed1afbbb1fd892272`. **`POME-SECTION-CURVRANK-METRIC-DEPENDENCE-001` — low / verified conditional deduction; no original theorem is refuted.** A fixed `n`-dimensional complex torus and **one fixed trivial line bundle** have smoothly varying Hermitian metrics with `r_h^+=r=0,...,n`, while `h^0(X,L^p)=1`, `kappa(L)=0`, and `chi(X,L^p tensor E)` is independent of `p`. # Coverage update — 2026-11-02 — zero-volume Okounkov boundary and locally nonintegrable separately subharmonic peaks

**Reviewed production main:** `b6933b772f41b45918f7e25ed1afbbb1fd892272`. **POME-OKO-ZEROVOL-HAUSDORFF-BOUNDARY-001** (**low / verified conditional deduction**) independently checks the current integrated §19.2 semigroup pseudometric and positive-volume Okounkov extension using two explicit full-lattice semigroup families in one pointed cone. Their `d_sg` distance is `2/N → 0`, yet their Okounkov-body Hausdorff distance is `1−1/N → 1`; they converge to the same zero-volume pseudometric element with different Hausdorff body limits. Thus the printed theorem is **correct** but neither globally uniformly continuous nor continuously extendable across volume zero; the positive-volume metric quotient is not complete. **Production main:** `b6933b772f41b45918f7e25ed1afbbb1fd892272`. **POME-BDIV-EXCEPTIONAL-TRACE-L1-JUMP-001 (low / verified conditional deduction)** newly directly examines the precise topology in §20.1.19 and regular-part Definition §20.1.14 (main `docs/sections/the-theory-of-b-divisors/index.html` blob `4a793dfd462badcb351f54a36cb030cde14a399b`, permanent tags `87D0CCEB6DF9`, `165D43352903`, lines 149–167 and 265–319). On `P²`, an elementary diagonal **projective-automorphism** family of smooth Kähler forms converges weakly with decreasing `L¹` potentials to a positive current with one isolated pole. **Root classes remain H**, but on the blowup the traces jump from `pi*H` to `pi*H-E`. Smooth top wedge probabilities tend to the point atom, while the limit **non-pluripolar** top mass is zero. This disproves replacing the printed `d_S` continuity hypothesis by weak-current/`L¹` convergence; it **does not** disprove the proposition. [Main-pinned full proof](reports/2026-11-02-bdiv-exceptional-trace-l1-discontinuity.md). - **POME-GAUD-SURFACE-VOLUME-RANGE-OPTIMIZERS-001 — low / verified conditional deduction.** For a compact complex surface the *smooth strictly positive potential masses* form a singleton if \(dd^c\omega=0\), otherwise a nondegenerate **open interval with no attained endpoint**; by the smooth normalized equation so do the smooth positive-probability measure-volumes. **Reviewed main:** `b8edb6d5fb0f26f5eae0b7b8c8b8d0230c66d9c6`. The table of contents has 25 chapters / 725 unique sections (inventory, not verification). The selected integrated proofs of §§12.7–12.9 and contextual arguments in §12.10 were directly read and compared, including permanent tags, source labels, line anchors and blob hashes in [reports/2026-10-22-gauduchon-weak-determinant-defect.md](reports/2026-10-22-gauduchon-weak-determinant-defect.md). New finding `POME-GAUD-DET-WEAK-DEFECT-001` is an **independently calculated sharpness counterexample to an UNASSERTED stronger continuity claim**, with conditional maximizing-current identification, status `verified conditional deduction / low`. No false printed theorem, no chapter certified. Oscillating smooth uniformly positive Gauduchon currents converge weakly, but their determinant measures converge to a strict fraction of the determinant measure of the limit current. This distinguishes Lemma 12.7.2 USC from continuity and validates the need for the objective-convergence premise in Lemma 12.8.1. The counterexample's direct analytical proof does not require regularization.
